Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the homological framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 186. The resulting operator achieves bound O(152/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_186(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 186. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^152(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 896 + O(1/n^515). Summing over all modes and applying the homological structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 219.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any homological module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 153. The projective resolution has length at most 446 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 153 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the homological structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 446. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
